The data used in the analysis were collected with the

BABAR detector at the PEP-II asymmetric-energy eþe?

collider at SLAC National Accelerator Laboratory. The

sample consists of 429 fb?1 of integrated luminosity

recorded at the ?ð4SÞ resonance mass (“on-peak”) and
page=6 block=-56007241 (x=264 y=321) width=245.000000 height=9.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
45 fb?1 collected 40 MeV below the resonance mass (“off-

peak”) [35]. The on-peak sample corresponds to the full

BABAR ?ð4SÞ data set and contains 470.9  2.8 million
page=6 block=-56007241 (x=264 y=357) width=245.000000 height=10.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
BB? events [30]. A detailed description of the BABAR

detector is given in Refs. [36,37]. Charged-particle tracks

We find that 20% of the remaining events in nonresonant

page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=189) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
applied except for those corresponding to a K? invariant-
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=201) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
mass veto described below, which is taken into account
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=213) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
separately. The 2D histogram of reconstructed MC events is
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=225) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
then divided by the 2D histogram of the generated MC
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=237) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
events. In order to expand regions of phase space with large
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=249) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
efficiency variations, the Dalitz plot variables are trans-
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=261) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
formed into “square Dalitz plot” [44] coordinates. We
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=273) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
obtain an average efficiency, for nonresonant MC events,
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=286) width=245.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
of approximately 15%. In the likelihood fit we use an
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=298) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
event-by-event efficiency that depends on the Dalitz plot
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=310) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
position.
page=8 block=16061900 (x=1 y=322) width=36.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=20
V. BB? BACKGROUNDS
page=8 block=496175144 (x=67 y=348) width=113.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNJ+AdvOT19ee2aa8.B wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
In addition to continuum events, background arises
page=8 block=16171610 (x=12 y=366) width=234.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
from nonsignal BB? events. A major source of BB? back-
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=378) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
ground arises from Bþ ? D?0ð?K0S?0Þ?þ decays. To sup-
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=387) width=245.000000 height=13.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
press this background, we veto events with 1.804< mK0S?0 <
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=403) width=245.000000 height=11.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
1.924GeV=c2.
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=417) width=62.000000 height=8.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=17
The remaining BB? backgrounds are studied using MC
page=8 block=16171610 (x=12 y=431) width=234.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
simulations and classified based on the shape of the mES,
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=443) width=245.000000 height=8.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
?E, and Dalitz plot distributions. We identify nine catego-
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=454) width=245.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
ries of BB? backgrounds: categories 1, 2 and 3 include
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=467) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
different types of three- and four-body B decays involving
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=480) width=245.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
an intermediate D meson; categories 4 and 5 include
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=492) width=245.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
charmless four-body B decays to intermediate resonances
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=504) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
where a ?0 inthe final state is not reconstructed; categories 6
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=515) width=245.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
and 7 include two-body B decays with a radiated photon
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=528) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
misreconstructed as a ?0 decay product or where the ?0
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=540) width=245.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
arises from the other B decay; category 8 includes charmless
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=553) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
three-body B decays where a charged pion is interchanged
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=565) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
with a ?0 meson from the other B; and finally category 9
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=577) width=245.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
includes all other simulated BB? background contributions.
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=591) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
Within each category, each of the mES, ?E, BDTout, and
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=602) width=245.000000 height=9.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
Dalitz plot distributions are formed by combining the
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=615) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
contributions of all decay modes in the category. The
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=627) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
combinations are done by normalizing the distributions
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=639) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
for each decay mode to the expected number of events in
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=651) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
the recorded data sample, which is estimated using
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=663) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=9
reconstruction efficiencies determined from MC, the
page=8 block=16171610 (x=1 y=675) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=10
number of BB? pairs in the recorded data sample, and
page=8 block=-56097543 (x=264 y=19) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
the branching fractions listed in Refs. [9,28]. For each
page=8 block=-56097543 (x=264 y=31) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
category, the histograms of mES, ?E, BDTout, and the Dalitz
page=8 block=-56097543 (x=264 y=42) width=245.000000 height=9.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
plot variables are used as the probability density functions
page=8 block=-56097543 (x=264 y=55) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
(PDF) in the likelihood fit to data to model the BB?
page=8 block=-56097543 (x=264 y=68) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
background.

for the change in the number of degrees of freedom when
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=297) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
the resonances ?ð1450Þþ, K2ð1430Þ0, K2ð1430Þþ,
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=305) width=245.000000 height=13.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=11
Kð1680Þ0, or Kð1680Þþ, are added to the default model
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=318) width=245.000000 height=10.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
one at a time, we find that the significance of each
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=334) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
resonance is well below 3 sigma. Therefore we do not
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=347) width=245.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
include any of these additional resonances in the
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=359) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=11
nominal fit. We also note that the fit fractions for these
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=371) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
additional resonances, reported in Table IV, are consistent
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=383) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
with zero.
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=395) width=43.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
We do not observe an excess of events for invariant
page=10 block=16065842 (x=12 y=407) width=234.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
masses greater than 2 GeV=c2, suggesting that a non-
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=418) width=245.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
resonant component, in addition to that included in the
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=431) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
LASS parametrization, is not necessary. We observe that
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=443) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
if we add a nonresonant component to the fit, the change
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=455) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
in log likelihood for the binned data and the fit
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=467) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=10
projections for the K0S?þ, K0S?0, and ?þ?0 invariant
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=476) width=245.000000 height=13.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=9
masses are consistent with the expected change due to
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=493) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
the additional free parameters in the fit, and do not
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=505) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
indicate any statistically significant nonresonant compo-
page=10 block=16065842 (x=1 y=517) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
nent. We therefore conclude that, with the current level

of statistical sensitivity, the base model, which includes

and asymmetries, by varying the fixed parameters. The

page=15 block=-56091058 (x=275 y=116) width=234.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
cancel to first order in the fit to CP asymmetries; therefore
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=128) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
the only uncertainties that are included for ACP are those
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=140) width=245.000000 height=8.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
coming from the fit and signal model. In addition to this, we
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=152) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
do not evaluate any of the uncertainties that are found to be
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=164) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
negligible for the branching fractions.
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=176) width=161.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
An additional uncertainty for ACP arises from having
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=275 y=188) width=234.000000 height=8.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
fixed the CP asymmetries for individual BB? background
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=201) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
components to zero. We vary the CP asymmetry indi-
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=213) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
vidually for each B background category, based on the
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=225) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
world-average experimental results [9], and take as
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=238) width=245.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=9
the corresponding uncertainty the sum in quadrature of
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=250) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
the largest of the positive or negative change in ACP.
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=262) width=232.000000 height=8.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
The uncertainty related to the efficiency model is
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=275 y=274) width=234.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=9
determined by exchanging the efficiency maps for the
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=286) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
positive and negative Dalitz plots and refitting the data. We
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=298) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
then take the difference in CP asymmetry with respect to
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=310) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
the nominal fit as the uncertainty.
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=322) width=144.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
We list in Table XIII the systematic uncertainties
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=275 y=334) width=234.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=9
associated with the signal CP asymmetries and the varia-
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=347) width=245.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
tions in the asymmetry due to changes in the signal
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=359) width=245.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
composition.
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=264 y=371) width=54.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=20
We evaluate systematic uncertainties for the CP ampli-
page=15 block=-56091058 (x=275 y=383) width=234.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
tudes and CP phases from the same sources as for the CP

asymmetries. We list the variations to the amplitudes Aþ in

page=15 block=-810873868 (x=297 y=510) width=179.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=DLLKNJ+AdvOT19ee2aa8.B wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6

are summarized in Table VIII, and the amplitude and phase

model uncertainties. We have measured for the first time

the branching fraction and CP asymmetry for the decay

violation in the intermediate decay Bþ ? Kð892Þþ?0,
page=15 block=-56018032 (x=264 y=598) width=245.000000 height=10.000000 fontname=DLLKNO+AdvOT483a8203 wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
with a total significance of 3.4 standard deviations deter-

mined by adding statistical, systematic, and signal-model

uncertainties in quadrature and dividing the measured ACP
